# MBTL script and data catalogue

Generated by `tools/squirrel/catalog.py mbtl <data dir>`; do not edit by hand. JSON: `mbtl_files.json`, `mbtl_names.json`. Translations are machine (`"tr":"machine"`) until reviewed; JP is kept beside them.

| path | Squirrel | load site | timing | hot reload | purpose | header (EN) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `System/PlayBonusTbl.txt` | yes |  | untraced | no | play bonus table | Value added depending on situation |
| `data/BattleInfo.txt` | yes | Boot_LoadGlobalResources -> BattleInfo_LoadGlobal 0x504B50 | boot | reloadstd | global battle tuning values, g_BattleInfo | Attacker side |
| `data/BoundEff.txt` | yes | Boot_LoadGlobalResources -> BoundEff_LoadGlobal 0x56C5D0 | boot | reloadstd | wall/ground bounce effect table, g_BoundEffTable | Desc: hitstun effect definition |
| `data/VectorTable.txt` | yes | Boot_LoadGlobalResources 0x84E7A0 -> VectorTable_LoadGlobal 0x4FE250 | boot | reloadstd | global vector (knockback/launch) table, g_VectorTable | vectorlist |
| `data/_combase.txt` | yes | Sq_InitBattleVM 0x7F4710 "_com_base_" | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | CPU AI base classes CCom/CComSkill/CComMove | - Always check that created actions produce no errors / - Tune COM at max difficulty / - Tech is done automatically internally. |
| `data/_combase_boss.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from <chr>_com_7/8/9.txt (boss COM) | chara load | reloadchara (rebuild only) | boss CPU AI base classes (pulled in by the variant com files) | - Always check that created actions produce no errors / - Tune COM at max difficulty / - Tech is done automatically internally. |
| `data/_combase_boss2.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from <chr>_com_7/8/9.txt (boss COM) | chara load | reloadchara (rebuild only) | boss CPU AI base classes (pulled in by the variant com files) | - Always check that created actions produce no errors / - Tune COM at max difficulty / - Tech is done automatically internally. |
| `data/_combase_boss9.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from <chr>_com_7/8/9.txt (boss COM) | chara load | reloadchara (rebuild only) | boss CPU AI base classes (pulled in by the variant com files) | Common motion combase that does almost nothing. Basically everything is done in per-character parts |
| `script/Battle_Init.txt` | yes | Sq_InitBattleVM 0x7F4710 -> Sq_RunScriptFile 0x815380 "BattleInit" | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | battle VM init: loads the btl_Define* constants and debug helpers | Debug output related |
| `script/Battle_Std.txt` | yes | Sq_InitBattleVM 0x7F4710 -> Sq_RunScriptFile "BattleScript" | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | battle VM std script: Battle_Std/BtlPl/BtlOb/BtlPt tables, pulls in the btl_* std files | For connection |
| `script/btl_ChrTableFunc.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | MakeMoveTable/MakeCommandTable: builds each character table from the std tables | If it persists while referenced, try cloning first / Nested tables weren't supported, so fixed to a proper deep copy |
| `script/btl_CmdCheck.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | command check helpers (CmdCheck evaluation) | MEMO |
| `script/btl_Define.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Init.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | system constants: Def_Sys_* version switches, def_* flags, pattern numbers | Adjustment |
| `script/btl_Define_Chara.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Init.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | character numbers (Def_ChrNo_*) and per-character constants | Character number |
| `script/btl_Define_CharaNew.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Init.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | additional character constants | Empty file |
| `script/btl_Define_NoLocalDebug.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Init.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | debug switches for release builds | Debug message output related |
| `script/btl_MvFunc.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | shared move functions (Battle_Std.* used by moves) | Definition of empty function |
| `script/btl_MvInitFunc.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | shared move Init helpers | Attack Init common function |
| `script/btl_SEDefine.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from <chr>_se_list.txt | chara load | reloadchara (rebuild only) | sound effect constants (loaded by each character se_list) | SEPrio constant-like values / is local scoped to this file only? |
| `script/btl_StdCommdandTbl.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | GetStdCommandTable: standard commands (dash, jump, guard, system actions) | Std_CommandTable <- {}; / done this way because tired of shallow copies |
| `script/btl_StdMoveTbl.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | GetStdMoveTable: standard moves (stand, walk, guard, hitstun, knockdown, system moves) | Very long function / _ChrNo: specify character number |
| `script/btl_StdNpcMoveTbl.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | standard NPC moves (story/talk NPC objects) | Very long function |
| `script/btl_Std_BattleStatusTbl.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | default Battle_Status values shared by characters | Standard battle status table / loaded per character SC |
| `script/btl_debugFunc.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Init.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | debug print helpers | debuglog.write_r("debug_log\n"); //just in case, on every startup |
| `script/cmn_Pertner.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | partner object helpers (assist/partner characters) | Functions for various partners |
| `script/cmn_SetPosition.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | round start position helpers | Various SetPosition functions |
| `script/sq_stagebgm.txt` | yes | __dofile__ from Battle_Std.txt | boot (battle VM) | reloadstd | stage BGM script (stage_bgm.initround) | Desc: stage_bgm.initround() / Set here if battle BGM changes; takes priority over BGM set in battle table / Invalid unless battle has started when called |

### script/btl_Define.txt (1794)

| name | value | EN | JP |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Def_Sys_ButtonDashSkillBouhatuCheck` | `1` | Address special moves leaking easily after 6AB dash (same as UNI) | 6ABダッシュのあと必殺技が漏れやすいのを対応(UNIと同じ) |
| `Def_Sys_SetHosyoHoseiToPartnerCharacter` | `1` | Also apply guaranteed damage compensation to partner character attacks (Mario's 236C etc. were not affected by guarantee compensation) | パートナーキャラの攻撃にも保証ダメージ補正をかける（マーリオゥの236Cなどが保証補正の影響を受けていなかった） |
| `Def_Sys_MultiJumpTrueFurimuki` | `1` | Properly turn around on double jump | 二段ジャンプ時にちゃんと振り向く |
| `Def_Sys_CheckAirGuardFunouHame` | `1` | Detect when an air-unblockable move is landed as a starter on a target that could air-guard | 空中ガード不能技を空中でガード可能なところに始動であてたときを検知 |
| `Def_Sys_ChangeMoonSkillCost` | `1` | Adjust the Moon consumption of Moon Skills | ムーンスキルの月消費量を調整する |
| `Def_Sys_KeepUkemiRapidRelayAtk` | `15` | Set a minimum unrecoverable value so Rapid Beat launcher reliably connects even at CP1 | CP1でもラピッドビートの打ち上げが確実につながるように受け身不能の最低値を入れる |
| `Def_Sys_CoomboMoonSkill_MinWarrantyVsDamage` | `400` | Chip a fixed amount of VS damage on Moon Skill hit (once per combo); value is chip damage | ムーンスキルヒット時にVSダメージを固定値削る（１コンボ中１回）　値は削りダメージ |
| `Def_Sys_SetGuardKakuteiHameHitHosei` | `1` | Apply a correction when an unblockable move hits during guard recovery | ガード不能技をガード硬直中に当てたときに補整をかける |
| `Def_Sys_CheckArmorFlag_EndParam2ArmorTiming` | `0` | When the armor designation in Param2 disappears, check the armor flag and handle it if the state looks buggy | Param2のアーマー指定が消えたタイミングでアーマーフラグをチェックして、バグってそうな状態だったら対応する |
| `Def_Sys_BossChara_MoonSkillVsAtkType` | `1` | Whether to make boss characters' Moon Skill VS coefficient the same as normal special moves (since CPU in Boss Rush becomes too strong) | ボスキャラのムーンスキルのVS係数を通常必殺技と同じにするか（ボスラッシュのCPUが強くなりすぎるので） |
| `Def_Sys_NerfShieldCounter` | `1` | Weaken Shield Counter | シールドカウンターを弱体化する |
| `Def_Sys_ChangeShieldCancelMoonCost` | `1` | No Moon decrease from Shield whiff on Shield cancel | シールドキャンセル時はシールド空振りでのムーン減少をなくす |
| `Def_Sys_ShieldCounterFrameCheckType` | `1` | Shield Cancel input window frames do not advance during own time stop | シルカンの受付フレームは自分の時間停止中は進めないようにする |
| `Def_Sys_ComboArcDrive_MoonAdd` | `2000` | Moon that increases when AD is incorporated into a combo | ADをコンボに組み込むと増える月 |
| `Def_Sys_SidouArcDrive_MoonAdd` | `1000` | Moon gained when AD is landed as the starter | ADを始動で当てたときに増える月 |
| `Def_Sys_ChargeBlast_MinusValuePar` | `95` | Coefficient for heat time reduction speed on charge release; directly affects recovery amount; 0 disables | タメ開放のヒート時間減少速度の係数　回復量にそのまま影響がでる　0で無効 |
| `Def_Sys_GuardFloat_AddMutekiFrame` | `0` | Whether to add invincibility time when double-jumping after guard float; deprecated; note the invincibility-removal handling is sloppy, so be careful if revived | ガードフロート後に二段ジャンプしたとき無敵時間を乗せるかどうか　廃止　※無敵時間を消す処理が雑なので復活時は注意 |
| `Def_Sys_GroShield_FromLandingAirShield` | `3` | Mid-air Shield just before landing transitions to ground Shield; frames of leeway; handling made limited | 着地間際の空中シールドは地上シールドへ移行する　※猶予F　※処理を限定的にした |
| `Def_Sys_EnableGuardJumpCancel_LunchExSkill` | `0` | Make an EX special that launches Jump-Cancelable (jc) when guarded | 打ち上げるタイプのEX必殺技をガードされたときにｊｃ可能にする |
| `Def_Sys_AtkAFinTimingNoRapidBeat` | `1` | Make the final frame of A attacks unable to Rapid Beat. *Counter to timed-press attacks becoming RB. *Skipped in 1.34 but applied in 1.40 | A攻撃の最終フレームはラピッドビート不可にする　※目押し攻撃がRBになるの対策　※1.34で見送ったが1.40で反映 |
| `Def_Sys_EraseObjHitBackFlags_UniqIDChange` | `1` | Projectiles and AtkObj clear _ObjFlags_ToParentHitBack when creator parent changes | 飛び道具とAtkObjは生成親が変わったタイミングで_ObjFlags_ToParentHitBackを消す |
| `Def_Sys_ShieldToObject_CheckEnemyHitStatus` | `1` | When an object is Shielded, if the player also has attack info, treat it as a simultaneous Shield and allow Shielded cancels etc. | オブジェクトをシールドしたとき、プレイヤーにも攻撃情報が入っていたら、同時シールドだと思うので、シールドされキャンセルなどを可能にする |
| `Def_Sys_FixStopAdv_ShieldSameTime` | `1` | When Shielding, e.g. by guarding twice within 1F, fix the hitstop recovery difference instead of calculating it | 1F内に2回ガードするなどして、シールドしたときは、ヒットストップの硬直差の計算をしないで固定化する |
| `Def_Sys_FixLandBoundCpHoseiBug` | `2` | 1: Fix the ground-bounce CP correction calculation bug (while slightly easing the untechable time) 2: Only add near-infinite countermeasures | 1:地面バウンドCP補正の計算の不具合を直す（直しつつ少し受け身不能時間を緩和する）　2:ほとんど永久対策だけ入れる |
| `Def_Sys_FixAddVectimeCpHoseiBug` | `1` | Restoring the CP tech-impossible coefficient to normal would make some parts not connect as before, so revert it | CPによる受け身不能の係数を正常に直してしまうと前よりつながらないパーツがでてくるのでもとに戻す |
| `Def_Sys_ShieldMissGuardNoPlusStop` | `1` | Do not insert time stop on failed-Shield guard (time stop would throw off the controls) | シールド失敗ガード時の時間停止を入れない（時間停止があると操作感がズレてしまうため） |
| `Def_Sys_LowAirShieldCancel_AddYVec` | `1` | Make low-altitude Shield counter float slightly higher to hold the frame disadvantage to -2F (so it isn't advantageous) | 低空シールド返しは少し高く浮くようにして硬直差を-2Fに抑える（有利にならないようにする） |
| `Def_Sys_RapidBeatCommandType` | `1` | Change Rapid Beat command spec 0: conventional 1: reflect character select changes | ラピッドビートのコマンド仕様を変える 0:従来 1:キャラセレの変更を反映する |
| `Def_Sys_AirDashAirGuard` | `1` | Air guard after air dash 0: cannot guard 1: can guard | 空中ダッシュのあとの空中ガード　0:ガード不可　1:ガード可 |
| `Def_Sys_3DanaJumpGuard` | `1` | Guardability after triple jump | ３段ジャンプ後のガード可否 |
| `Def_Sys_DashGuard` | `1` | Whether guard is possible while dashing | ダッシュ走っているときのガード可否 |
| `Def_Sys_DashStartupAct` | `0` | Whether dash startup becomes actionable (and whether to delay dash voice start timing slightly) | ダッシュでかかりを行動可能にするか(＋ダッシュ音声開始タイミングをやや遅らせるか) |
| `Def_Sys_AirAtkXVecLimit` | `0` | Vector limit when performing a jump attack 1.2X:3500 | ジャンプ攻撃を出すときのベクトル制限 1.2X:3500 |
| `Def_Sys_BackAttackSousai` | `0` | Whether to clash when hit from behind 0: no clash (affects normals only) | 背後からの被弾時に相殺するかどうか 0:相殺しない（影響は通常技のみ） |
| `Def_Sys_CatchClashType` | `1` | Parry clash 0: old handling 1: | 当て身相殺　0:旧処理　1: |
| `Def_Sys_NerfShield_CheckPosStatus` | `1` | Shield nerf: standing Shield fails to crouching BC, crouching Shield fails to standing BC (guard + chip + Moon decrease) | シールドの弱体化　立ちシールドはしゃがみBC、屈シールドは立ちBCでシールド失敗になる（ガード＋削り＋月減少） |
| `Def_Sys_NerfShield_ChangeDirectFrame` | `1` | Shield nerf: raw Shield gains extra recovery *Not implemented | シールドの弱体化　生シールドは硬直が増加　※未実装 |
| `Def_Sys_AerialAirAtk_UkemiLimit` | `11` | Raise the lower limit of untechable time for jump attacks in air combos | 空中コンボのジャンプ攻撃の受け身不能時間の下限をあげる |
| `Def_Sys_HoldShield_UseCost` | `1` | Whether to reduce Moon while Shield is held | 押しっぱなしシールドで月が減っていく処理をするか |
| `Def_Sys_MoonSkill_GaugeLimitType` | `2` | 0:1.2x spec 1:MS gain limit loosened 2:no limit | 0:1.2x仕様 1:MS増加制限がゆるくなる 2:制限なし |
| `Def_Sys_SidouSkillBonus` | `1` | Starter and its follow-ups receive only the Skill Bonus benefit and do not consume it | 始動技とそこからの派生はスキルボーナスの恩恵だけ受けて消費しない |
| `Def_Sys_Del_ThorowRejectMiss` | `1` | Remove throw-fail frames after throw input | 投げ入力後の投げ失敗フレームをなくす |
| `Def_Sys_MoonDrive_EnableContinuousAirDash` | `1` | Allow dashing in the same direction, e.g. air dash > air dash during MD (processing other than Aoko; consecutive air dashes not allowed) | MD中の空中ダッシュ＞空中ダッシュなどの同じ方向へのダッシュを許容する(青子以外の処理。また空ダの連続は不可) |
| `Def_Sys_SousaiCancelType` | `1` | Clash performance adjustment; 1: can jump cancel when hitboxes clash with each other | 相殺の性能調整 1:攻撃判定同士相殺のときjc可能に |
| `Def_Sys_ShieldedCancelExMuteki` | `1` | Make a Shielded BC stay invincible until the player can act | シールドされBCは行動可能になるまで無敵が続くようにする |
| `Def_Sys_AlignFrame_DashStopAndNoAtk` | `1` | Can act from dash = can use moves = stops, so dash-cancel-dash setups are disabled | ダッシュから行動可能になる＝技が出せる＝停止するようになる、とする（ダッシュ仕込みダッシュをできなくする） |
| `Def_Sys_AllSkillBonus` | `1` | Process where, even without a Skill Bonus, landing a special move increases untechable time according to CP (except invincible moves) | スキルボーナスが無くても、必殺技を当てるとCPに応じて受け身不能時間が増える処理(無敵技以外) |
| `Def_Sys_AllBoundUkemiBonus` | `0` | Process where bouncing increases untechable time according to CP (normals and specials only; except invincible moves) | バウンドさせるとCPに応じて受け身不能時間が増える処理(通常技・必殺技のみ。無敵技以外) |
| `Def_Sys_ShieldCancelFrameBonus` | `1` | On Shield cancel, Shield recovery is reduced (currently only air Shield landing recovery) | シールドキャンセル時はシールドの硬直が減る(今のところ空中シールドの着地硬直のみ) |
| `Def_Sys_SousaiLifeRecoverOnlyMoonSkill` | `1` | Health recovery from clash happens only when caught by a Moon Skill; clash types are attack hitbox vs hitbox, BE move clash, and MS move clash | 相殺で体力が回復するのはムーンスキルで受け止めたときだけにする　攻撃判定同士の相殺、BE技の相殺、MS技の相殺がある |
| `Def_Sys_AirAtkSousaiPlusWeakFrame` | `2` | Disadvantage frames added by clash on jump-attack types | ジャンプ攻撃系の相殺で増やす不利フレーム |
| `Def_Sys_ChangeShieldEffect_EasyToSeeEnd` | `1` | Make Shield end timing easy to see by the size of the Shield effect | シールド終了タイミングをシールドエフェクトのサイズでわかりやすくする |
| `Def_Sys_ShieldSuccessGuardStatusType` | `1` | Shield success Mv state; 0: guard switching allowed, 1: guard switching not allowed but can re-deploy with D | シールド成功MVの状態　0:ガード切り替え可　1:ガード切り替え不可だが・再度Dで貼り直せる |
| `Def_Sys_JumpAtkHitStop_JumpCanceledNoChange` | `1` | Remove the processing that reduces jump attack hitstop after jump cancel etc. | ジャンプキャンセル後などにジャンプ攻撃のヒットストップをへらす処理をなくす |
| `Def_Sys_NoShieldCancel_DelayNoGuard` | `2` | For raw Shield, make some frames of the latter half of recovery guard-only into recovery | 生シールドの場合、硬直後半のガードのみ可能なフレームを少し硬直にする |
| `Def_Sys_LowAtkStart_DamageHosei` | `0` | Base correction applied when starting with A-type moves (overwrite correction; no correction on Fatal) | A系統始動時にかける基底補正（上書き補正、フェイタル時は補正がかからない） |
| `Def_Sys_ComboRateSetStartTiming` | `1` | Change combo rate application from Init to Start timing | コンボレートをかけるのをInitではなくStartタイミングに変更する |
| `Def_Sys_ChangeSousaiJumpCancelFrame` | `1` | Adjust takeoff frames when 3C and ScA are cancelled by clash jump cancel | 3CとScAを相殺ジャンキャンしたとき離陸フレームを調整する |
| `Def_Sys_3CSousaiJumpCancelWaitFrame` | `3` | Takeoff frames when 3C is clanged and jump-canceled (only occurs on 3C anti-air) and extra hitstop frames when 3C clashes with a jump attack | 3Cを相殺ジャンキャンしたとき離陸フレーム（3C対空時のみ発生）　＆3Cをジャンプ攻撃と相殺したときの追加時間停止F |
| `Def_Sys_GroScSousaiJumpCancelWaitFrame` | `4` | When doing Cancel-A on Shield-cancel via clang jump cancel, takeoff frames. Note: character differences would be unfair, too short is too strong; since 1.35 the scA side clash is 1F shorter so add 1F | シルカンAを相殺ジャンキャンしたとき離陸フレーム　※キャラ差があるとかわいそう＆短すぎると強すぎる　1.35からscA側の相殺が1F短くなってるので1F追加 |
| `Def_Sys_KeepActive_ScHitToPartner` | `1` | Whether to treat a Shield-cancel that hits the partner as not hitting and rerun processing so it hits the player again | シルカンがパートナーにあたったとき、あたってないことにしてプレイヤーに再度あたるように処理を動かすか |
| `Def_Sys_DrawBonusAnnounce` | `0` | Whether to display skill bonus and bounce bonus | スキルボーナスやバウンスボーナスを表示するかどうか |
| `Def_Sys_EnableShieldHoldCmd_InEnemyAnten` | `1` | Under blackout, holding D allows Shield | 被暗転時はD押しっぱなしでシールドが出せるようになる |
| `Def_Sys_ExtendAirAtk_LandDelayPlusFrame` | `4` | Increase landing recovery on whiffed charged jump attack | タメジャンプ攻撃の空振り時の着地硬直を増やす |
| `Def_Sys_ChangeExtraBackStepMuteki` | `5` | Back step invincibility adjustment; 1: invincible +2F, only head and projectile invincibility extended further | バクステ無敵の調整　1:無敵+2F＆頭無敵と弾無敵だけさらに延長 |
| `Def_Sys_TruncationThrowFDashVec` | `1` | Do not apply inertia when ground throw dash motion is 0F (limited to when cornered) | 地上投げのダッシュモーションが0Fのときは慣性をのせない（端背負い時のみに限定） |
| `Def_Sys_AtoThrowF_CheckBouhatu` | `1` | Add a countermeasure for Shield leaking easily when going A-series > throw | A系統＞投げとしたときにシールドが漏れやすい対策を入れる |
| `Def_Sys_ShieldedToThrowF_CheckBouhatu` | `10` | Add a countermeasure for Shield leaking easily when doing a no-cancel throw after being Shielded *the Shielding side cannot | シールド取られたあとにノーキャン投げとしたときにシールドが漏れやすい対策を入れる　※取った側は無理 |
| `Def_Sys_ShieldMissGuard_LastNoNageMuteki` | `1` | Remove throw invincibility on failed-Shield guard escape (there is throw invincibility during guard so it is not a guaranteed hit, but makes for a disadvantageous mind game) | シールド失敗ガード抜けの投げ無敵をなくす（ガード中は投げ無敵があるので確定はしないが、不利な読み合いになる） |

| `Def_Sys_MoonDriveModoriMutekiType` | `0` | Recovery after MD activation 0: old spec (invincible at -2F) 1: new spec | MD発動後の硬直 0:旧仕様(-2Fで無敵あり) 1:新仕様 |
